       </p>\n204\t      )}\n205\t\n206\t      {/* Hidden file input — image only, no PDF */}\n207\t      <input\n208\t        ref={inputRef}\n209\t        id={id}\n210\t        type=\"file\"\n211\t )\n229\t}\n230\t\n231\tfunction inputCls(hasError) {\n232\t  return `h-12 px-4 rounded-lg border ${\n233\t    hasError ?
